# The carries — every open hand-off, VERBATIM, with its receipts

<!-- ⛔ THIS FILE IS A DESTINATION, NOT A DRAFT. Nothing here is authored twice: every line
     below arrived by a `_gm_move.py` MOVE from a ★ LATEST banner, byte-identical, or was
     written straight here by the wrap instead of onto the banner. Newest session FIRST.
     A cold session does NOT read this file. It reads `_CHAIN.md`, whose banner names this
     file and its count; it comes here only when it needs the bodies, and it is CHEAPER to
     fetch one section than to carry all of them at boot:
       `python3 knowledge/_memento_search.py "<q>"` → `--fetch carries:residual-<N>` -->

> **WHY THIS FILE EXISTS — `s225-D2` (Dave, 2026-08-30), and the number that forced it.**
> The carry list lived as ONE line on `GOOD-MORNING.md`'s ★ LATEST banner. At #225 that line
> measured **24,873 tape — 89.3% of the whole banner**. Because `_gen_chain.py` slices the
> banner OUT of GM to build `_CHAIN.md`, the line sat on **both sides** of that generator's
> chain/GM ratio assertion, which is why the ratio read **45,656 / 86,879 = 52.5%** against a
> **`<40%`** floor and took CI red at build step 13. Dave's word: *the chain carry list moves
> to its own generated home with receipts; the banner carries a pointer + count.*
> ⛔ **THE FLOOR DID NOT MOVE AND NO CARRY WAS DROPPED.** Both `s214-D6` commands are
> satisfied at once — a shorter banner AND a complete carry set — because the list MOVED.

> **THE CONTRACT, four clauses, so a future wrap cannot get this wrong:**
> **1. VERBATIM OR NOT AT ALL.** A carry arrives here by `_gm_move.py` (`{"op": "move"}`) or is
> written here first; it is never summarised, re-worded, re-ordered or truncated on the way in.
> The `s128-D2` **age bracket**, the `s183-D1` **strike form** and the `s188-D2` **retraction
> receipt** all still apply — this is the same record in a different file, not a lighter one.
> **2. NEWEST FIRST**, one `## residual → #N` section per wrap, mirroring `_GM-ARCHIVE.md`.
> **3. THE BANNER GETS A POINTER AND A COUNT, AND THE COUNT IS PROBEABLE** — never a typed
> figure a reader has to trust [[roll-pointer-is-not-an-absence]]. The count is the repo's own
> definition of a carry, `_capture_gate._carry_items()`, run against the line in THIS file:
> `python3 -c "import sys;sys.path.insert(0,'knowledge');import _capture_gate as c;print(len(c._carry_items(next(l for l in open('_CARRIES.md',encoding='utf-8') if '**residual → #N:**' in l))))"`
> **4. NOTHING IS DELETED HERE, EVER.** A consumed carry is struck in place with its receipt,
> exactly as it would have been on the banner. This file only grows; when it becomes the cost
> the banner used to be, that is a measurement to take to Dave, not a licence to trim.

> ⚠ **KNOWN AND DECLARED AT BIRTH, not discovered later:** `carry_wording_check()` in
> `knowledge/_capture_gate.py` (BLOCKING, `s188-D2`) still pairs carries between the **★ LATEST
> and ★ PRIOR residual lines of `GOOD-MORNING.md`**. With the lists here it finds nothing to
> pair. At #225 it says so out loud (*"NOTHING PAIRED — ⛔ THAT IS NOT A PASS"*); from #226,
> with both banners carrying pointers, it goes **quiet**, which is the worse state. Re-pointing
> it at this file is a one-function change and it is OWED — see
> `notes/_subreports/2026-08-30-225-carries-home.md`. ⛔ Do not read this file's existence as
> that gate being satisfied.

Recorded as a measurement, not smoothed: the declaration was made in chat before the brief was cut, which is the #221 remedy working, and the overrun is still an overrun. ⛔ **No advisory, stop line or wall was moved.** · ⛔ **⑪ WHAT THIS WRAP DID NOT DO, NAMED RATHER THAN SMOOTHED [1]** — nothing on the DO-NOT-RULE fence was crossed: `knowledge/_rulings.json` **READ ONLY, never written by this seat** (`git status` CLEAN — the mechanical proof) · **no row of Dave's closed, reworded or re-scoped**, `W-217` expressly stays open · no constant, band, advisory, stop line, wall or lane moved · no advisory promoted · **no version, roster or `RATIFY_IDS` edit** · nothing un-parked · **step 3 (memory) SKIPPED BY THIS SEAT AND DECLARED — it is the conductor's**, and the memory store is invisible to every gate, so this is the one beat no receipt can cover. · ⚠ **⑫ CONSEQUENCES AND PITFALLS — MANDATORY (Dave #165), TODAY'S OWN [1]**. **(a)** ⛔ **THE FORENSICS BRIEF'S OWN PREMISE WAS FALSE AND THE SUB FOUND IT BY PROBING, NOT BY TRUSTING:** the shipped `_MANIFEST.json` carries **NO per-file shas** (schema `apollo-designer-pack-manifest/1`, fields `groups[].{group,key,paths,files,bytes}`), so the sub built its own sha baseline from the shipped zip [[premise-ages-faster-than-rule]]. **(b)** ⚠ **THE PACKER WAS NEVER THE BLOCKER — THE REPO'S OWN `.gitignore` WAS.** The ship list is generated from `git ls-tree`, so an ignored path can never ship however the manifest rules are written; two lines of negation were required, not one, because git does not descend into an excluded directory. **(c)** ⚠ **`FIRST-SESSION.md:51` CARRIED THE `s223-D8` STALE-DIRECTORY DEFECT AGAIN** and was swept in the same commit — fix the class, and then check the class did not regrow. **(d)** ✅ **THE ENACTMENT SUB STOPPED AT ITS FENCE** rather than rationalise a chain gone stale under the conductor's in-flight inscriptions, and reported; the conductor regenerated and committed. **(e)** ⚠ **THE #223 SIZE STAMP AND THE SELFTEST DISAGREE BY UNIT, NOT BY DRIFT** — `_gm_usage.py --sizes` reports GM **127,318 real** while `_gen_chain.py` measures the same file at **81,111 tk cl100k**. Two instruments, two units, neither wrong; **never convert one into the other** [[measure-dont-convert-units]]. · ⬛ **① ~~HIS FIFTEEN MINUTES IN COPILOT: THE ONLY REAL PROOF v1.0.2 HAS, AND IT IS UNBLOCKED NOW~~ [2, DAVE'S]** — ⛔ **STRUCK AT #224 WITH ITS RECEIPT: HE RAN IT, AND THE FORENSICS SETTLED WHAT HE RAN.** The pack he tested is **v1.0.2**, settled by sha (all 34 files differing between v1.0.1 and v1.0.2 match v1.0.2; both the `s222-D2` and `s222-D3` files present with matching shas), and **the encoder tiers WORKED** — the purepy fallback engaged under a reproduced corp-blocked tiktoken and returned an exact cl100k count, his own chain stamped at the cl100k tier. `W-244` **CLOSED on his word (“close”)**, `closed_by` carrying the receipt.
